合成実験：まず初期状態で、延べ入力が減っても費用が増えることを確認する。次に割引を0%へ変え、順位がどう変わるかを見る。削除位置を後ろへ動かすと、再利用できる先頭部分が長くなる。再取得を足すと、読取り結果だけでなく追加呼出しの出力も課金される。これらは仮定した記号列の計算であり、モデルの成功率を予測しない。

"prompt":["S1","S2","T1.1","T1.2","T1.3","T1.4","T2.1","T2.2","T2.3","T2.4","T3.1","T3.2","T3.3","T3.4","T4.1","T4.2","T4.3","T4.4","T5.1","T5.2","T5.3","T5.4"],"n":22,"c":18,"new":4,"rebilled":0,"output":2,"first_changed":null,"cost":14}],"total":82,"tokens":72}},{"args":{"discount":1,"position":3,"timing":6,"retrieval":4},"rewrite_call":null,"removed":[],"baseline":{"rows":[{"prompt":["S1","S2"],"n":2,"c":0,"new":2,"rebilled":0,"output":2,"first_changed":null,"cost":12},{"prompt":["S1","S2","T1.1","T1.2","T1.3","T1.4"],"n":6,"c":2,"new":4,"rebilled":0,"output":2,"first_changed":null,"cost":14},{"prompt":["S1","S2","T1.1","T1.2","T1.3","T1.4","T2.1","T2.2","T2.3","T2.4"],"n":10,"c":6,"new":4,"rebilled":0,"output":2,"first_changed":null,"cost":14},{"prompt":["S1","S2","T1.1","T1.2","T1.3","T1.4","T2.1","T2.2","T2.3","T2.4","T3.1","T3.2","T3.3","T3.4"],"n":14,"c":10,"new":4,"rebilled":0,"output":2,"first_changed":null,"cost":14},{"prompt":["S1","S2","T1.1","T1.2","T1.3","T1.4","T2.1","T2.2","T2.3","T2.4","T3.1","T3.2","T3.3","T3.4","T4.1","T4.2","T4.3","T4.4"],"n":18,"c":14,"new":4,"rebilled":0,"output":2,"first_changed":null,"cost":14},{"prompt":["S1","S2","T1.1","T1.2","T1.3","T1.4","T2.1","T2.2","T2.3","T2.4","T3.1","T3.2","T3.3","T3.4","T4.1","T4.2","T4.3","T4.4","T5.1","T5.2","T5.3","T5.4"],"n":22,"c":18,"new":4,"rebilled":0,"output":2,"first_changed":null,"cost":14}],"total":82,"tokens":72},"changed":{"rows":[{"prompt":["S1","S2"],"n":2,"c":0,"new":2,"rebilled":0,"output":2,"first_changed":null,"cost":12},{"prompt":["S1","S2","T1.1","T1.2","T1.3","T1.4"],"n":6,"c":2,"new":4,"rebilled":0,"output":2,"first_changed":null,"cost":14},{"prompt":["S1","S2","T1.1","T1.2","T1.3","T1.4","T2.1","T2.2","T2.3","T2.4"],"n":10,"c":6,"new":4,"rebilled":0,"output":2,"first_changed":null,"cost":14},{"prompt":["S1","S2","T1.1","T1.2","T1.3","T1.4","T2.1","T2.2","T2.3","T2.4","T3.1","T3.2","T3.3","T3.4"],"n":14,"c":10,"new":4,"rebilled":0,"output":2,"first_changed":null,"cost":14},{"prompt":["S1","S2","T1.1","T1.2","T1.3","T1.4","T2.1","T2.2","T2.3","T2.4","T3.1","T3.2","T3.3","T3.4","T4.1","T4.2","T4.3","T4.4"],"n":18,"c":14,"new":4,"rebilled":0,"output":2,"first_changed":null,"cost":14},{"prompt":["S1","S2","T1.1","T1.2","T1.3","T1.4","T2.1","T2.2","T2.3","T2.4","T3.1","T3.2","T3.3","T3.4","T4.1","T4.2","T4.3","T4.4","T5.1","T5.2","T5.3","T5.4"],"n":22,"c":18,"new":4,"rebilled":0,"output":2,"first_changed":null,"cost":14}],"total":82,"tokens":72}}]}</script><script>(()=>{
'use strict';
const data=JSON.parse(document.getElementById('deletion-data').textContent).synthetic;
const ids=['discount','position','timing','retrieval'];
const controls=ids.map(id=>document.getElementById('deletion-'+id));
const initial=[.9,1,4,0];
const fmt=x=>x.toFixed(2);
function render(){
 const args=controls.map(x=>Number(x.value));
 const s=data.find(s=>ids.every((id,i)=>s.args[id]===args[i]));
 if(!s) throw new Error('Missing teaching case');
 const b=s.baseline,c=s.changed;
 document.getElementById('deletion-output').innerHTML=`<p>削除なし <strong data-baseline>${fmt(b.total)}</strong> ／ 削除あり <strong data-changed>${fmt(c.total)}</strong> ／ 差 <strong data-delta>${fmt(c.total-b.total)}</strong> 費用単位</p><p>延べ入力：${b.tokens} → ${c.tokens}トークン。削除実施：${s.rewrite_call===null?'なし':s.rewrite_call+'回目の通常呼出し'}。成功率は計算しません。</p><p>緑＝再利用／橙＝残っているのに再課金／灰＝新規。枠内の1記号が1トークンです。</p>`+
 '<div class="deletion-ledger"><table><caption>削除側の呼出しごとの台帳（追加読取りも1行）</caption><thead><tr><th>呼出し</th><th>入力列と再利用境界</th><th>n / c</th><th>再課金</th><th>出力</th><th>費用</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+c.rows.map((r,i)=>{
 const prev=i?c.rows[i-1].prompt:[];
 const strip=r.prompt.map((t,j)=>`<span class="token ${j<r.c?'cached':prev.slice(r.c).includes(t)?'rebilled':'new'}">${j===r.first_changed?'↓':''}${t}</span>`).join('');
 return `<tr><th>${i+1}</th><td><div class="tokens">${strip}</div><small>${r.first_changed===null?'共通部分の途中に変更なし':'最初の相違：'+(r.first_changed+1)+'番目'}</small></td><td>${r.n} / ${r.c}</td><td>${r.rebilled}</td><td>${r.output}</td><td>${fmt(r.cost)}</td></tr>`;
 }).join('')+'</tbody></table></div>';
}
controls.forEach(c=>c.addEventListener('change',render));
document.getElementById('deletion-reset').addEventListener('click',()=>{controls.forEach((c,i)=>c.value=String(initial[i]));render();});
render();
})();
</script>

### この実験だけの仮定

先頭のS1・S2と、各ターン4個の記号が入力になる。T1〜T3のうち一つから中央2記号を1回だけ削る。削除前後の通常呼出しは各6回、出力は各2記号。選んだターンがまだ存在しなければ削除を待つ。T1を4回目に削除する例ではT2とT3を保持し、論文の2ターン保持を説明できる。ただしtoyは全ターンへ順次適用する著者の介入ではない。

追加読取りは、削除直後にR記号を取得する呼出しを1回挿入する仮定。出力1記号と取得した入力、その後の再送を加算する。再取得の量や生起確率を論文から推定していない。削除注記や要約生成の費用も省いている。1記号＝1人工トークンであり、文章の文字数やモデル固有tokenizerとは対応しない。
